- **2021 priority classification:** should_know
- **OBEC pathway classification:** terminal
- **Official indicator:** ปฏิบัติตนในศาสนพิธี พิธีกรรม และวันสำคัญทางศาสนา ตามที่กำหนดได้ถูกต้อง

**Objective:** `SO1.2-P2-02` — ปฏิบัติตนในศาสนพิธี พิธีกรรม และวันสำคัญทางศาสนา ตามที่กำหนดได้ถูกต้อง
- **Learning intention:** Learners will demonstrate: ปฏิบัติตนในศาสนพิธี พิธีกรรม และวันสำคัญทางศาสนา ตามที่กำหนดได้ถูกต้อง
**Suggested teaching sequence**

1. Begin with a familiar situation involving kindness, honesty, responsibility or respectful coexistence; invite learners to describe the action without disclosing personal beliefs.
2. Present a short, balanced source about the relevant tradition, teaching or practice. Identify its context and distinguish factual information from personal opinion.
3. Model how the source connects to the indicator. In pairs, compare two possible actions and explain which better demonstrates the stated principle while respecting different beliefs.
4. Learners complete the application task independently and explain the evidence behind their choice. Assess understanding and respectful reasoning, never personal faith or worship.

**Resources:** a short age-appropriate account, a neutral information card and a real-life ethical situation.

**Differentiation:** For early primary learners: Use large pictures, concrete objects, short oral instructions and teacher modelling; allow learners to point, sort, draw or answer orally.

**Learning intention:** Learners will be able to demonstrate the specific knowledge or performance in this official indicator: ปฏิบัติตนในศาสนพิธี พิธีกรรม และวันสำคัญทางศาสนา ตามที่กำหนดได้ถูกต้อง

**Practical activity:** Use a respectful account or case from an appropriate tradition; connect the relevant teaching, value or practice to a real-life choice without assessing personal belief. Adjust the complexity to early primary learners and use a source or response format appropriate to their age.

**Expected evidence:** Three completed responses demonstrate SO1.2-P2-02: accurate identification, a clear explanation with a relevant example, and a grade-appropriate application task. Accept oral, written, visual, map-based, timeline-based or practical evidence where it measures the same target.

**Teacher check:** Check separately (1) accuracy against the Thai indicator, (2) explanation supported by an example or source, and (3) application to the scenario. Give feedback on the missing criterion.

**Assessment stimulus — verified tradition/source card:** Use a teacher-verified sequence/information card for one curriculum-specified ceremony, ritual or important day. Include its purpose, key actions and observer/participant conduct.

**Independent practice — indicator-specific assessment tasks**
1. **Recall / evidence:** Name the event and identify or sequence its key actions from the card. This assesses: “ปฏิบัติตนในศาสนพิธี พิธีกรรม และวันสำคัญทางศาสนา ตามที่กำหนดได้ถูกต้อง”. **Answer:** Event and actions are accurately identified.
2. **Explain / compare:** Explain the purpose of one action using the source note. This task assesses: “ปฏิบัติตนในศาสนพิธี พิธีกรรม และวันสำคัญทางศาสนา ตามที่กำหนดได้ถูกต้อง”. **Answer:** Purpose matches the verified source.
3. **Apply / demonstrate:** In a scenario based on the verified event card, demonstrate or sequence appropriate conduct for a participant or observer and explain why it fits that event. This task assesses: “ปฏิบัติตนในศาสนพิธี พิธีกรรม และวันสำคัญทางศาสนา ตามที่กำหนดได้ถูกต้อง”. **Answer:** Conduct and explanation match the named event and source; observing is acceptable and worship participation is never compulsory.### 41. SO2.1-P2-01

- **Standard:** SO2.1
- **2021 priority classification:** must_know
- **OBEC pathway classification:** terminal
- **Official indicator:** ปฏิบัติตนตามข้อตกลง กติกา กฎ ระเบียบและหน้าที่ ที่ต้องปฏิบัติในชีวิตประจำวัน

**Objective:** `SO2.1-P2-01` — ปฏิบัติตนตามข้อตกลง กติกา กฎ ระเบียบและหน้าที่ ที่ต้องปฏิบัติในชีวิตประจำวัน
- **Learning intention:** Learners will demonstrate: ปฏิบัติตนตามข้อตกลง กติกา กฎ ระเบียบและหน้าที่ ที่ต้องปฏิบัติในชีวิตประจำวัน
**Suggested teaching sequence**

1. Introduce a realistic school or community situation in which a right, duty, rule or public responsibility matters. Ask who is affected.
2. Read the case and relevant rule/process together. Identify the people involved, the right or duty at stake, and the authority or process that applies.
3. Model a stakeholder chart. Groups compare possible responses for fairness, rights, responsibilities and consequences, citing the relevant rule.
4. Each learner proposes and justifies a fair, lawful next step. Check that the recommendation protects affected people and uses the relevant civic process.

**Resources:** a school/community case card, a stakeholder chart and the relevant child-rights, school-rule or civic-process extract.

**Differentiation:** For early primary learners: Use large pictures, concrete objects, short oral instructions and teacher modelling; allow learners to point, sort, draw or answer orally.

**Learning intention:** Learners will be able to demonstrate the specific knowledge or performance in this official indicator: ปฏิบัติตนตามข้อตกลง กติกา กฎ ระเบียบและหน้าที่ ที่ต้องปฏิบัติในชีวิตประจำวัน

**Practical activity:** Present a realistic school or community case; identify people involved, rights, duties, rules or civic processes, and a fair next step. Adjust the complexity to early primary learners and use a source or response format appropriate to their age.

**Expected evidence:** Three completed responses demonstrate SO2.1-P2-01: accurate identification, a clear explanation with a relevant example, and a grade-appropriate application task. Accept oral, written, visual, map-based, timeline-based or practical evidence where it measures the same target.

**Teacher check:** Check separately (1) accuracy against the Thai indicator, (2) explanation supported by an example or source, and (3) application to the scenario. Give feedback on the missing criterion.

**Assessment stimulus — school/community case card:** Use a school/community rules card with a real or fictional daily situation, the relevant rule and the reason it exists.

**Independent practice — indicator-specific assessment tasks**
1. **Recall / evidence:** Identify the rule that applies to the situation. This assesses: “ปฏิบัติตนตามข้อตกลง กติกา กฎ ระเบียบและหน้าที่ ที่ต้องปฏิบัติในชีวิตประจำวัน”. **Answer:** Selects the relevant rule from the card.
2. **Explain / compare:** Explain how following the rule protects people or supports shared activity. This task assesses: “ปฏิบัติตนตามข้อตกลง กติกา กฎ ระเบียบและหน้าที่ ที่ต้องปฏิบัติในชีวิตประจำวัน”. **Answer:** Explains its purpose in context.
3. **Apply / demonstrate:** Complete a short classroom routine simulation by following the relevant agreement/rule, then point to the rule that guided the action. This task assesses: “ปฏิบัติตนตามข้อตกลง กติกา กฎ ระเบียบและหน้าที่ ที่ต้องปฏิบัติในชีวิตประจำวัน”. **Answer:** The learner performs the action consistently with the stated rule and distinguishes it from a personal preference.### 42. SO2.1-P2-02

- **Standard:** SO2.1
- **2021 priority classification:** should_know
- **OBEC pathway classification:** between_learning
- **Official indicator:** ปฏิบัติตนตามมารยาทไทย

**Objective:** `SO2.1-P2-02` — ปฏิบัติตนตามมารยาทไทย
- **Learning intention:** Learners will demonstrate: ปฏิบัติตนตามมารยาทไทย
**Suggested teaching sequence**

1. Introduce a realistic school or community situation in which a right, duty, rule or public responsibility matters. Ask who is affected.
2. Read the case and relevant rule/process together. Identify the people involved, the right or duty at stake, and the authority or process that applies.
3. Model a stakeholder chart. Groups compare possible responses for fairness, rights, responsibilities and consequences, citing the relevant rule.
4. Each learner proposes and justifies a fair, lawful next step. Check that the recommendation protects affected people and uses the relevant civic process.

**Resources:** a school/community case card, a stakeholder chart and the relevant child-rights, school-rule or civic-process extract.

**Differentiation:** For early primary learners: Use large pictures, concrete objects, short oral instructions and teacher modelling; allow learners to point, sort, draw or answer orally.

**Learning intention:** Learners will be able to demonstrate the specific knowledge or performance in this official indicator: ปฏิบัติตนตามมารยาทไทย

**Practical activity:** Present a realistic school or community case; identify people involved, rights, duties, rules or civic processes, and a fair next step. Adjust the complexity to early primary learners and use a source or response format appropriate to their age.

**Expected evidence:** Three completed responses demonstrate SO2.1-P2-02: accurate identification, a clear explanation with a relevant example, and a grade-appropriate application task. Accept oral, written, visual, map-based, timeline-based or practical evidence where it measures the same target.

**Teacher check:** Check separately (1) accuracy against the Thai indicator, (2) explanation supported by an example or source, and (3) application to the scenario. Give feedback on the missing criterion.

**Assessment stimulus — school/community case card:** Use picture or dialogue cards showing culturally appropriate Thai manners in several contexts (greeting, speaking, listening, visiting); verify the examples with local teachers.

**Independent practice — indicator-specific assessment tasks**
1. **Recall / evidence:** Identify the context and the respectful manner shown in each picture/dialogue. This assesses: “ปฏิบัติตนตามมารยาทไทย”. **Answer:** Matches conduct to the context shown.
2. **Explain / compare:** Explain why the manner is appropriate in that context. This task assesses: “ปฏิบัติตนตามมารยาทไทย”. **Answer:** Explains respect for the other person and situation.
3. **Apply / demonstrate:** Role-play a respectful response to a new, realistic school/community interaction; the observer records the specific behaviour shown rather than judging accent, status or personality. This task assesses: “ปฏิบัติตนตามมารยาทไทย”. **Answer:** The behaviour is appropriate to the stated context and respects personal boundaries; accept culturally and situationally appropriate variation.### 43. SO2.1-P2-03

- **Standard:** SO2.1
- **2021 priority classification:** should_know
- **OBEC pathway classification:** terminal
- **Official indicator:** แสดงพฤติกรรมในการยอมรับความคิด ความเชื่อ และการปฏิบัติของบุคคลอื่นที่แตกต่างกันโดยปราศจากอคติ

**Objective:** `SO2.1-P2-03` — แสดงพฤติกรรมในการยอมรับความคิด ความเชื่อ และการปฏิบัติของบุคคลอื่นที่แตกต่างกันโดยปราศจากอคติ
- **Learning intention:** Learners will demonstrate: แสดงพฤติกรรมในการยอมรับความคิด ความเชื่อ และการปฏิบัติของบุคคลอื่นที่แตกต่างกันโดยปราศจากอคติ
**Suggested teaching sequence**

1. Introduce a realistic school or community situation in which a right, duty, rule or public responsibility matters. Ask who is affected.
2. Read the case and relevant rule/process together. Identify the people involved, the right or duty at stake, and the authority or process that applies.
3. Model a stakeholder chart. Groups compare possible responses for fairness, rights, responsibilities and consequences, citing the relevant rule.
4. Each learner proposes and justifies a fair, lawful next step. Check that the recommendation protects affected people and uses the relevant civic process.

**Resources:** a school/community case card, a stakeholder chart and the relevant child-rights, school-rule or civic-process extract.

**Differentiation:** For early primary learners: Use large pictures, concrete objects, short oral instructions and teacher modelling; allow learners to point, sort, draw or answer orally.

**Learning intention:** Learners will be able to demonstrate the specific knowledge or performance in this official indicator: แสดงพฤติกรรมในการยอมรับความคิด ความเชื่อ และการปฏิบัติของบุคคลอื่นที่แตกต่างกันโดยปราศจากอคติ

**Practical activity:** Present a realistic school or community case; identify people involved, rights, duties, rules or civic processes, and a fair next step. Adjust the complexity to early primary learners and use a source or response format appropriate to their age.

**Expected evidence:** Three completed responses demonstrate SO2.1-P2-03: accurate identification, a clear explanation with a relevant example, and a grade-appropriate application task. Accept oral, written, visual, map-based, timeline-based or practical evidence where it measures the same target.

**Teacher check:** Check separately (1) accuracy against the Thai indicator, (2) explanation supported by an example or source, and (3) application to the scenario. Give feedback on the missing criterion.

**Assessment stimulus — school/community case card:** Use two fictional profiles showing different opinions, beliefs or practices, with a shared classroom/community activity.

**Independent practice — indicator-specific assessment tasks**
1. **Recall / evidence:** Identify one difference between the two profiles without labelling either as better. This assesses: “แสดงพฤติกรรมในการยอมรับความคิด ความเชื่อ และการปฏิบัติของบุคคลอื่นที่แตกต่างกันโดยปราศจากอคติ”. **Answer:** States a factual difference from the profiles without stereotyping.
2. **Explain / compare:** Explain how both people can take part in the shared activity respectfully. This task assesses: “แสดงพฤติกรรมในการยอมรับความคิด ความเชื่อ และการปฏิบัติของบุคคลอื่นที่แตกต่างกันโดยปราศจากอคติ”. **Answer:** Suggests inclusive participation for both people.
3. **Apply / demonstrate:** In a paired discussion, demonstrate listening to a different view, restating it fairly and responding without ridicule or exclusion. This task assesses: “แสดงพฤติกรรมในการยอมรับความคิด ความเชื่อ และการปฏิบัติของบุคคลอื่นที่แตกต่างกันโดยปราศจากอคติ”. **Answer:** Teacher observes accurate listening and a non-discriminatory response; learners are not required to agree with the other view.### 44. SO2.1-P2-04

- **Standard:** SO2.1
- **2021 priority classification:** must_know
- **OBEC pathway classification:** between_learning
- **Official indicator:** เคารพในสิทธิ เสรีภาพของตนเองและผู้อื่น

**Objective:** `SO2.1-P2-04` — เคารพในสิทธิ เสรีภาพของตนเองและผู้อื่น
- **Learning intention:** Learners will demonstrate: เคารพในสิทธิ เสรีภาพของตนเองและผู้อื่น
**Suggested teaching sequence**

1. Introduce a realistic school or community situation in which a right, duty, rule or public responsibility matters. Ask who is affected.
2. Read the case and relevant rule/process together. Identify the people involved, the right or duty at stake, and the authority or process that applies.
3. Model a stakeholder chart. Groups compare possible responses for fairness, rights, responsibilities and consequences, citing the relevant rule.
4. Each learner proposes and justifies a fair, lawful next step. Check that the recommendation protects affected people and uses the relevant civic process.

**Resources:** a school/community case card, a stakeholder chart and the relevant child-rights, school-rule or civic-process extract.

**Differentiation:** For early primary learners: Use large pictures, concrete objects, short oral instructions and teacher modelling; allow learners to point, sort, draw or answer orally.

**Learning intention:** Learners will be able to demonstrate the specific knowledge or performance in this official indicator: เคารพในสิทธิ เสรีภาพของตนเองและผู้อื่น

**Practical activity:** Present a realistic school or community case; identify people involved, rights, duties, rules or civic processes, and a fair next step. Adjust the complexity to early primary learners and use a source or response format appropriate to their age.

**Expected evidence:** Three completed responses demonstrate SO2.1-P2-04: accurate identification, a clear explanation with a relevant example, and a grade-appropriate application task. Accept oral, written, visual, map-based, timeline-based or practical evidence where it measures the same target.

**Teacher check:** Check separately (1) accuracy against the Thai indicator, (2) explanation supported by an example or source, and (3) application to the scenario. Give feedback on the missing criterion.

**Assessment stimulus — school/community case card:** Use a rights-and-responsibilities scenario card for a child at home and school; include a fair example where one person's action affects another's rights.

**Independent practice — indicator-specific assessment tasks**
1. **Recall / evidence:** Identify one right and one responsibility the learner has in the case. This assesses: “เคารพในสิทธิ เสรีภาพของตนเองและผู้อื่น”. **Answer:** Correctly identifies rights and responsibilities from the case.
2. **Explain / compare:** Explain how respecting another person's right changes what the learner should do. This task assesses: “เคารพในสิทธิ เสรีภาพของตนเองและผู้อื่น”. **Answer:** Explains the effect on another person's right.
3. **Apply / demonstrate:** Resolve the case by naming a fair action that protects both the learner's and others' rights. This task assesses: “เคารพในสิทธิ เสรีภาพของตนเองและผู้อื่น”. **Answer:** Proposes a fair, safe action that respects both parties.
### 45. SO2.2-P2-01

- **Standard:** SO2.2
- **2021 priority classification:** should_know
- **OBEC pathway classification:** between_learning
- **Official indicator:** อธิบายความสัมพันธ์ของตนเองและสมาชิกในครอบครัวในฐานะเป็นส่วนหนึ่งของชุมชน

**Objective:** `SO2.2-P2-01` — อธิบายความสัมพันธ์ของตนเองและสมาชิกในครอบครัวในฐานะเป็นส่วนหนึ่งของชุมชน
- **Learning intention:** Learners will demonstrate: อธิบายความสัมพันธ์ของตนเองและสมาชิกในครอบครัวในฐานะเป็นส่วนหนึ่งของชุมชน
**Suggested teaching sequence**

1. Introduce a realistic school or community situation in which a right, duty, rule or public responsibility matters. Ask who is affected.
2. Read the case and relevant rule/process together. Identify the people involved, the right or duty at stake, and the authority or process that applies.
3. Model a stakeholder chart. Groups compare possible responses for fairness, rights, responsibilities and consequences, citing the relevant rule.
4. Each learner proposes and justifies a fair, lawful next step. Check that the recommendation protects affected people and uses the relevant civic process.

**Resources:** a school/community case card, a stakeholder chart and the relevant child-rights, school-rule or civic-process extract.

**Differentiation:** For early primary learners: Use large pictures, concrete objects, short oral instructions and teacher modelling; allow learners to point, sort, draw or answer orally.

**Learning intention:** Learners will be able to demonstrate the specific knowledge or performance in this official indicator: อธิบายความสัมพันธ์ของตนเองและสมาชิกในครอบครัวในฐานะเป็นส่วนหนึ่งของชุมชน

**Practical activity:** Present a realistic school or community case; identify people involved, rights, duties, rules or civic processes, and a fair next step. Adjust the complexity to early primary learners and use a source or response format appropriate to their age.

**Expected evidence:** Three completed responses demonstrate SO2.2-P2-01: accurate identification, a clear explanation with a relevant example, and a grade-appropriate application task. Accept oral, written, visual, map-based, timeline-based or practical evidence where it measures the same target.

**Teacher check:** Check separately (1) accuracy against the Thai indicator, (2) explanation supported by an example or source, and (3) application to the scenario. Give feedback on the missing criterion.

**Assessment stimulus — school/community case card:** Use a simple relationship map linking a child/family to community groups and services, with an example of mutual support.

**Independent practice — indicator-specific assessment tasks**
1. **Recall / evidence:** Identify two links between the family and the wider community in the map. This assesses: “อธิบายความสัมพันธ์ของตนเองและสมาชิกในครอบครัวในฐานะเป็นส่วนหนึ่งของชุมชน”. **Answer:** Links match the map.
2. **Explain / compare:** Explain how one community relationship benefits both the family and community. This task assesses: “อธิบายความสัมพันธ์ของตนเองและสมาชิกในครอบครัวในฐานะเป็นส่วนหนึ่งของชุมชน”. **Answer:** Explains a reciprocal or supported benefit.
3. **Apply / demonstrate:** Suggest one appropriate way a family member can contribute to a community activity. This task assesses: “อธิบายความสัมพันธ์ของตนเองและสมาชิกในครอบครัวในฐานะเป็นส่วนหนึ่งของชุมชน”. **Answer:** Contribution is realistic and age-appropriate.
### 46. SO2.2-P2-02

- **Standard:** SO2.2
- **2021 priority classification:** should_know
- **OBEC pathway classification:** between_learning
- **Official indicator:** ระบุผู้มีบทบาท อำนาจในการตัดสินใจในโรงเรียนและชุมชน

**Objective:** `SO2.2-P2-02` — ระบุผู้มีบทบาท อำนาจในการตัดสินใจในโรงเรียนและชุมชน
- **Learning intention:** Learners will demonstrate: ระบุผู้มีบทบาท อำนาจในการตัดสินใจในโรงเรียนและชุมชน
**Suggested teaching sequence**

1. Introduce a realistic school or community situation in which a right, duty, rule or public responsibility matters. Ask who is affected.
2. Read the case and relevant rule/process together. Identify the people involved, the right or duty at stake, and the authority or process that applies.
3. Model a stakeholder chart. Groups compare possible responses for fairness, rights, responsibilities and consequences, citing the relevant rule.
4. Each learner proposes and justifies a fair, lawful next step. Check that the recommendation protects affected people and uses the relevant civic process.

**Resources:** a school/community case card, a stakeholder chart and the relevant child-rights, school-rule or civic-process extract.

**Differentiation:** For early primary learners: Use large pictures, concrete objects, short oral instructions and teacher modelling; allow learners to point, sort, draw or answer orally.

**Learning intention:** Learners will be able to demonstrate the specific knowledge or performance in this official indicator: ระบุผู้มีบทบาท อำนาจในการตัดสินใจในโรงเรียนและชุมชน

**Practical activity:** Present a realistic school or community case; identify people involved, rights, duties, rules or civic processes, and a fair next step. Adjust the complexity to early primary learners and use a source or response format appropriate to their age.

**Expected evidence:** Three completed responses demonstrate SO2.2-P2-02: accurate identification, a clear explanation with a relevant example, and a grade-appropriate application task. Accept oral, written, visual, map-based, timeline-based or practical evidence where it measures the same target.

**Teacher check:** Check separately (1) accuracy against the Thai indicator, (2) explanation supported by an example or source, and (3) application to the scenario. Give feedback on the missing criterion.

**Assessment stimulus — school/community case card:** Use a verified school/community organisation chart or fictional decision case showing who has authority over different kinds of decisions.

**Independent practice — indicator-specific assessment tasks**
1. **Recall / evidence:** Identify the person or group responsible for the decision in the case. This assesses: “ระบุผู้มีบทบาท อำนาจในการตัดสินใจในโรงเรียนและชุมชน”. **Answer:** Correctly identifies the decision-maker shown.
2. **Explain / compare:** Explain why that person or group has a role in this decision, using the chart or case. This task assesses: “ระบุผู้มีบทบาท อำนาจในการตัดสินใจในโรงเรียนและชุมชน”. **Answer:** Reason follows the stated role/authority, not assumptions.
3. **Apply / demonstrate:** For a second issue, identify the appropriate decision-maker and explain what information they should consider. This task assesses: “ระบุผู้มีบทบาท อำนาจในการตัดสินใจในโรงเรียนและชุมชน”. **Answer:** Selects a relevant authority and identifies suitable evidence or stakeholder views.
### 47. SO3.1-P2-01

- **Standard:** SO3.1
- **2021 priority classification:** should_know
- **OBEC pathway classification:** between_learning
- **Official indicator:** ระบุทรัพยากรที่นำมาผลิตสินค้าและบริการที่ใช้ในชีวิตประจำวัน

**Objective:** `SO3.1-P2-01` — ระบุทรัพยากรที่นำมาผลิตสินค้าและบริการที่ใช้ในชีวิตประจำวัน
- **Learning intention:** Learners will demonstrate: ระบุทรัพยากรที่นำมาผลิตสินค้าและบริการที่ใช้ในชีวิตประจำวัน
**Suggested teaching sequence**

1. Present a familiar choice involving needs, wants, limited resources, work, goods, services or money. Ask learners what must be decided.
2. Identify the resources and constraints in the case. Model the relevant economic concept using a simple table, picture, calculation or flow diagram.
3. Pairs compare at least two choices, considering costs, benefits, opportunity, scarcity or effects on people as required by the indicator.
4. Learners complete an individual decision or recommendation and justify it with evidence from the case. Assess the economic reasoning, not merely the selected option.

**Resources:** a household, school or community decision card, a simple budget/resource table and a price, needs or production example.

**Differentiation:** For early primary learners: Use large pictures, concrete objects, short oral instructions and teacher modelling; allow learners to point, sort, draw or answer orally.

**Learning intention:** Learners will be able to demonstrate the specific knowledge or performance in this official indicator: ระบุทรัพยากรที่นำมาผลิตสินค้าและบริการที่ใช้ในชีวิตประจำวัน

**Practical activity:** Use a familiar household, workplace or community decision; identify resources, choices, costs, benefits or economic relationships. Adjust the complexity to early primary learners and use a source or response format appropriate to their age.

**Expected evidence:** Three completed responses demonstrate SO3.1-P2-01: accurate identification, a clear explanation with a relevant example, and a grade-appropriate application task. Accept oral, written, visual, map-based, timeline-based or practical evidence where it measures the same target.

**Teacher check:** Check separately (1) accuracy against the Thai indicator, (2) explanation supported by an example or source, and (3) application to the scenario. Give feedback on the missing criterion.

**Assessment stimulus — decision card:** Use a product/service production card listing the natural, human and capital resources used to make or deliver one familiar item/service.

**Independent practice — indicator-specific assessment tasks**
1. **Recall / evidence:** Identify two resources used in producing the item or service. This assesses: “ระบุทรัพยากรที่นำมาผลิตสินค้าและบริการที่ใช้ในชีวิตประจำวัน”. **Answer:** Resources match the production card.
2. **Explain / compare:** Sort the listed resources into the categories taught in class. This task assesses: “ระบุทรัพยากรที่นำมาผลิตสินค้าและบริการที่ใช้ในชีวิตประจำวัน”. **Answer:** Classification follows the categories explicitly taught.
3. **Apply / demonstrate:** Explain how changing or lacking one resource could affect production or service delivery. This task assesses: “ระบุทรัพยากรที่นำมาผลิตสินค้าและบริการที่ใช้ในชีวิตประจำวัน”. **Answer:** Explains a plausible production effect supported by the case.
### 48. SO3.1-P2-02

- **Standard:** SO3.1
- **2021 priority classification:** must_know
- **OBEC pathway classification:** between_learning
- **Official indicator:** บอกที่มาของรายได้และรายจ่ายของตนเองและครอบครัว

**Objective:** `SO3.1-P2-02` — บอกที่มาของรายได้และรายจ่ายของตนเองและครอบครัว
- **Learning intention:** Learners will demonstrate: บอกที่มาของรายได้และรายจ่ายของตนเองและครอบครัว
**Suggested teaching sequence**

1. Present a familiar choice involving needs, wants, limited resources, work, goods, services or money. Ask learners what must be decided.
2. Identify the resources and constraints in the case. Model the relevant economic concept using a simple table, picture, calculation or flow diagram.
3. Pairs compare at least two choices, considering costs, benefits, opportunity, scarcity or effects on people as required by the indicator.
4. Learners complete an individual decision or recommendation and justify it with evidence from the case. Assess the economic reasoning, not merely the selected option.

**Resources:** a household, school or community decision card, a simple budget/resource table and a price, needs or production example.

**Differentiation:** For early primary learners: Use large pictures, concrete objects, short oral instructions and teacher modelling; allow learners to point, sort, draw or answer orally.

**Learning intention:** Learners will be able to demonstrate the specific knowledge or performance in this official indicator: บอกที่มาของรายได้และรายจ่ายของตนเองและครอบครัว

**Practical activity:** Use a familiar household, workplace or community decision; identify resources, choices, costs, benefits or economic relationships. Adjust the complexity to early primary learners and use a source or response format appropriate to their age.

**Expected evidence:** Three completed responses demonstrate SO3.1-P2-02: accurate identification, a clear explanation with a relevant example, and a grade-appropriate application task. Accept oral, written, visual, map-based, timeline-based or practical evidence where it measures the same target.

**Teacher check:** Check separately (1) accuracy against the Thai indicator, (2) explanation supported by an example or source, and (3) application to the scenario. Give feedback on the missing criterion.

**Assessment stimulus — decision card:** Use a fictional household income-and-expense card with clearly labelled sources of income and common expense categories; no learner's private financial data.

**Independent practice — indicator-specific assessment tasks**
1. **Recall / evidence:** Identify two sources of income and two expenses shown in the example. This assesses: “บอกที่มาของรายได้และรายจ่ายของตนเองและครอบครัว”. **Answer:** Identifies correct examples.
2. **Explain / compare:** Sort each listed item as income or expense and explain one classification. This task assesses: “บอกที่มาของรายได้และรายจ่ายของตนเองและครอบครัว”. **Answer:** Classifies each item accurately.
3. **Apply / demonstrate:** Explain one way a household can plan expenses in relation to income using the fictional example. This task assesses: “บอกที่มาของรายได้และรายจ่ายของตนเองและครอบครัว”. **Answer:** Planning suggestion is feasible and respects the example's amounts.
### 49. SO3.1-P2-03

- **Standard:** SO3.1
- **2021 priority classification:** must_know
- **OBEC pathway classification:** between_learning
- **Official indicator:** บันทึกรายรับ-รายจ่ายของตนเอง

- **Learning intention:** Learners will demonstrate: บันทึกรายรับ-รายจ่ายของตนเอง
**Suggested teaching sequence**

1. Present a familiar choice involving needs, wants, limited resources, work, goods, services or money. Ask learners what must be decided.
2. Identify the resources and constraints in the case. Model the relevant economic concept using a simple table, picture, calculation or flow diagram.
3. Pairs compare at least two choices, considering costs, benefits, opportunity, scarcity or effects on people as required by the indicator.
4. Learners complete an individual decision or recommendation and justify it with evidence from the case. Assess the economic reasoning, not merely the selected option.

**Resources:** a household, school or community decision card, a simple budget/resource table and a price, needs or production example.

**Differentiation:** For early primary learners: Use large pictures, concrete objects, short oral instructions and teacher modelling; allow learners to point, sort, draw or answer orally.

**Learning intention:** Learners will be able to demonstrate the specific knowledge or performance in this official indicator: บันทึกรายรับ-รายจ่ายของตนเอง

**Practical activity:** Use a familiar household, workplace or community decision; identify resources, choices, costs, benefits or economic relationships. Adjust the complexity to early primary learners and use a source or response format appropriate to their age.

**Expected evidence:** Three completed responses demonstrate SO3.1-P2-03: accurate identification, a clear explanation with a relevant example, and a grade-appropriate application task. Accept oral, written, visual, map-based, timeline-based or practical evidence where it measures the same target.

**Teacher check:** Check separately (1) accuracy against the Thai indicator, (2) explanation supported by an example or source, and (3) application to the scenario. Give feedback on the missing criterion.

**Assessment stimulus — decision card:** Use a fictional learner's dated income/expense ledger with complete, simple amounts and a starting balance.

**Independent practice — indicator-specific assessment tasks**
1. **Recall / evidence:** Read the ledger and identify one income item and one expense item. This assesses: “บันทึกรายรับ-รายจ่ายของตนเอง”. **Answer:** Correctly identifies items.
2. **Explain / compare:** Calculate the total income and total expenses for the period. This task assesses: “บันทึกรายรับ-รายจ่ายของตนเอง”. **Answer:** Totals are arithmetically correct.
3. **Apply / demonstrate:** Complete a dated practice ledger from supplied, age-appropriate transactions, recording each income/expense and calculating the closing balance; use fictional amounts if preferred. This task assesses: “บันทึกรายรับ-รายจ่ายของตนเอง”. **Answer:** Every transaction is entered in the correct column and the balance is arithmetically correct; no learner must disclose personal finances.### 50. SO3.1-P2-04

- **Standard:** SO3.1
- **2021 priority classification:** must_know
- **OBEC pathway classification:** terminal
- **Official indicator:** สรุปผลดีของการใช้จ่ายที่เหมาะสมกับรายได้และการออม

**Objective:** `SO3.1-P2-04` — สรุปผลดีของการใช้จ่ายที่เหมาะสมกับรายได้และการออม
- **Learning intention:** Learners will demonstrate: สรุปผลดีของการใช้จ่ายที่เหมาะสมกับรายได้และการออม
**Suggested teaching sequence**

1. Present a familiar choice involving needs, wants, limited resources, work, goods, services or money. Ask learners what must be decided.
2. Identify the resources and constraints in the case. Model the relevant economic concept using a simple table, picture, calculation or flow diagram.
3. Pairs compare at least two choices, considering costs, benefits, opportunity, scarcity or effects on people as required by the indicator.
4. Learners complete an individual decision or recommendation and justify it with evidence from the case. Assess the economic reasoning, not merely the selected option.

**Resources:** a household, school or community decision card, a simple budget/resource table and a price, needs or production example.

**Differentiation:** For early primary learners: Use large pictures, concrete objects, short oral instructions and teacher modelling; allow learners to point, sort, draw or answer orally.

**Learning intention:** Learners will be able to demonstrate the specific knowledge or performance in this official indicator: สรุปผลดีของการใช้จ่ายที่เหมาะสมกับรายได้และการออม

**Practical activity:** Use a familiar household, workplace or community decision; identify resources, choices, costs, benefits or economic relationships. Adjust the complexity to early primary learners and use a source or response format appropriate to their age.

**Expected evidence:** Three completed responses demonstrate SO3.1-P2-04: accurate identification, a clear explanation with a relevant example, and a grade-appropriate application task. Accept oral, written, visual, map-based, timeline-based or practical evidence where it measures the same target.

**Teacher check:** Check separately (1) accuracy against the Thai indicator, (2) explanation supported by an example or source, and (3) application to the scenario. Give feedback on the missing criterion.

**Assessment stimulus — decision card:** Use a fictional budget showing income, necessary expenses, optional spending and a possible saving amount.

**Independent practice — indicator-specific assessment tasks**
1. **Recall / evidence:** Identify the income and necessary expenses in the budget. This assesses: “สรุปผลดีของการใช้จ่ายที่เหมาะสมกับรายได้และการออม”. **Answer:** Correctly identifies budget elements.
2. **Explain / compare:** Calculate what remains after necessary expenses. This task assesses: “สรุปผลดีของการใช้จ่ายที่เหมาะสมกับรายได้และการออม”. **Answer:** Calculation is correct.
3. **Apply / demonstrate:** Explain one benefit of the saving choice and identify one risk of spending more than the remaining amount. This task assesses: “สรุปผลดีของการใช้จ่ายที่เหมาะสมกับรายได้และการออม”. **Answer:** Explains a relevant benefit and overspending risk.
### 51. SO3.2-P2-01

- **Standard:** SO3.2
- **2021 priority classification:** should_know
- **OBEC pathway classification:** between_learning
- **Official indicator:** อธิบายการแลกเปลี่ยนสินค้าและบริการโดยวิธีต่าง ๆ

**Objective:** `SO3.2-P2-01` — อธิบายการแลกเปลี่ยนสินค้าและบริการโดยวิธีต่าง ๆ
- **Learning intention:** Learners will demonstrate: อธิบายการแลกเปลี่ยนสินค้าและบริการโดยวิธีต่าง ๆ
**Suggested teaching sequence**

1. Present a familiar choice involving needs, wants, limited resources, work, goods, services or money. Ask learners what must be decided.
2. Identify the resources and constraints in the case. Model the relevant economic concept using a simple table, picture, calculation or flow diagram.
3. Pairs compare at least two choices, considering costs, benefits, opportunity, scarcity or effects on people as required by the indicator.
4. Learners complete an individual decision or recommendation and justify it with evidence from the case. Assess the economic reasoning, not merely the selected option.

**Resources:** a household, school or community decision card, a simple budget/resource table and a price, needs or production example.

**Differentiation:** For early primary learners: Use large pictures, concrete objects, short oral instructions and teacher modelling; allow learners to point, sort, draw or answer orally.

**Learning intention:** Learners will be able to demonstrate the specific knowledge or performance in this official indicator: อธิบายการแลกเปลี่ยนสินค้าและบริการโดยวิธีต่าง ๆ

**Practical activity:** Use a familiar household, workplace or community decision; identify resources, choices, costs, benefits or economic relationships. Adjust the complexity to early primary learners and use a source or response format appropriate to their age.

**Expected evidence:** Three completed responses demonstrate SO3.2-P2-01: accurate identification, a clear explanation with a relevant example, and a grade-appropriate application task. Accept oral, written, visual, map-based, timeline-based or practical evidence where it measures the same target.

**Teacher check:** Check separately (1) accuracy against the Thai indicator, (2) explanation supported by an example or source, and (3) application to the scenario. Give feedback on the missing criterion.

**Assessment stimulus — decision card:** Use two simple cases of exchange (money purchase and direct exchange/barter) with the goods/services and terms clearly stated.

**Independent practice — indicator-specific assessment tasks**
1. **Recall / evidence:** Identify what is being exchanged in each case. This assesses: “อธิบายการแลกเปลี่ยนสินค้าและบริการโดยวิธีต่าง ๆ”. **Answer:** Correctly identifies exchanged goods/services or money.
2. **Explain / compare:** Compare how the two exchange methods work and identify one benefit or limitation of each. This task assesses: “อธิบายการแลกเปลี่ยนสินค้าและบริการโดยวิธีต่าง ๆ”. **Answer:** Comparison is accurate and context-based.
3. **Apply / demonstrate:** Choose a suitable exchange method for a new scenario and justify it. This task assesses: “อธิบายการแลกเปลี่ยนสินค้าและบริการโดยวิธีต่าง ๆ”. **Answer:** Choice fits the new case with a defensible reason.
### 52. SO3.2-P2-02

- **Standard:** SO3.2
- **2021 priority classification:** should_know
- **OBEC pathway classification:** terminal
- **Official indicator:** บอกความสัมพันธ์ระหว่างผู้ซื้อและผู้ขาย

**Objective:** `SO3.2-P2-02` — บอกความสัมพันธ์ระหว่างผู้ซื้อและผู้ขาย
- **Learning intention:** Learners will demonstrate: บอกความสัมพันธ์ระหว่างผู้ซื้อและผู้ขาย
**Suggested teaching sequence**

1. Present a familiar choice involving needs, wants, limited resources, work, goods, services or money. Ask learners what must be decided.
2. Identify the resources and constraints in the case. Model the relevant economic concept using a simple table, picture, calculation or flow diagram.
3. Pairs compare at least two choices, considering costs, benefits, opportunity, scarcity or effects on people as required by the indicator.
4. Learners complete an individual decision or recommendation and justify it with evidence from the case. Assess the economic reasoning, not merely the selected option.

**Resources:** a household, school or community decision card, a simple budget/resource table and a price, needs or production example.

**Differentiation:** For early primary learners: Use large pictures, concrete objects, short oral instructions and teacher modelling; allow learners to point, sort, draw or answer orally.

**Learning intention:** Learners will be able to demonstrate the specific knowledge or performance in this official indicator: บอกความสัมพันธ์ระหว่างผู้ซื้อและผู้ขาย

**Practical activity:** Use a familiar household, workplace or community decision; identify resources, choices, costs, benefits or economic relationships. Adjust the complexity to early primary learners and use a source or response format appropriate to their age.

**Expected evidence:** Three completed responses demonstrate SO3.2-P2-02: accurate identification, a clear explanation with a relevant example, and a grade-appropriate application task. Accept oral, written, visual, map-based, timeline-based or practical evidence where it measures the same target.

**Teacher check:** Check separately (1) accuracy against the Thai indicator, (2) explanation supported by an example or source, and (3) application to the scenario. Give feedback on the missing criterion.

**Assessment stimulus — decision card:** Use a labelled transaction case identifying buyer, seller, product/service, price and the responsibilities of each party.

**Independent practice — indicator-specific assessment tasks**
1. **Recall / evidence:** Identify the buyer, seller and item/service in the transaction. This assesses: “บอกความสัมพันธ์ระหว่างผู้ซื้อและผู้ขาย”. **Answer:** Correctly identifies roles and transaction.
2. **Explain / compare:** Explain one responsibility of the buyer and one of the seller. This task assesses: “บอกความสัมพันธ์ระหว่างผู้ซื้อและผู้ขาย”. **Answer:** Responsibilities are accurate and balanced.
3. **Apply / demonstrate:** Resolve a transaction problem fairly by stating what each party should do. This task assesses: “บอกความสัมพันธ์ระหว่างผู้ซื้อและผู้ขาย”. **Answer:** Fair solution respects agreed terms and consumer/seller responsibilities.
### 53. SO4.1-P2-01

- **Standard:** SO4.1
- **2021 priority classification:** should_know
- **OBEC pathway classification:** between_learning
- **Official indicator:** ใช้คำระบุเวลาที่แสดงเหตุการณ์ในอดีต ปัจจุบัน และอนาคต

**Objective:** `SO4.1-P2-01` — ใช้คำระบุเวลาที่แสดงเหตุการณ์ในอดีต ปัจจุบัน และอนาคต
- **Learning intention:** Learners will demonstrate: ใช้คำระบุเวลาที่แสดงเหตุการณ์ในอดีต ปัจจุบัน และอนาคต
**Suggested teaching sequence**

1. Display an age-appropriate historical image, object or event prompt. Ask learners what they notice and what they still need evidence to establish.
2. Model how to identify source type, author/context, date and relevant detail. Separate what the source directly shows from an inference or opinion.
3. Learners sequence events or compare two sources, recording details that support a cause, consequence, change, continuity or historical interpretation relevant to the indicator.
4. Each learner writes, explains or presents a short conclusion supported by source evidence. Check chronology and whether the evidence actually supports the claim.

**Resources:** a dated photograph or artefact image, a short primary/secondary source extract and a timeline or source-analysis frame.

**Differentiation:** For early primary learners: Use large pictures, concrete objects, short oral instructions and teacher modelling; allow learners to point, sort, draw or answer orally.

**Learning intention:** Learners will be able to demonstrate the specific knowledge or performance in this official indicator: ใช้คำระบุเวลาที่แสดงเหตุการณ์ในอดีต ปัจจุบัน และอนาคต

**Practical activity:** Use an age-appropriate historical source, image or timeline; identify evidence, sequence events and distinguish evidence from opinion. Adjust the complexity to early primary learners and use a source or response format appropriate to their age.

**Expected evidence:** Three completed responses demonstrate SO4.1-P2-01: accurate identification, a clear explanation with a relevant example, and a grade-appropriate application task. Accept oral, written, visual, map-based, timeline-based or practical evidence where it measures the same target.

**Teacher check:** Check separately (1) accuracy against the Thai indicator, (2) explanation supported by an example or source, and (3) application to the scenario. Give feedback on the missing criterion.

**Assessment stimulus — local-history evidence set:** Use a three-part event strip with clearly labelled past, present and future events and time expressions.

**Independent practice — indicator-specific assessment tasks**
1. **Recall / evidence:** Sort the supplied event statements into past, present and future. This assesses: “ใช้คำระบุเวลาที่แสดงเหตุการณ์ในอดีต ปัจจุบัน และอนาคต”. **Answer:** All statements are sorted correctly.
2. **Explain / compare:** Underline the time word that helped identify each event. This task assesses: “ใช้คำระบุเวลาที่แสดงเหตุการณ์ในอดีต ปัจจุบัน และอนาคต”. **Answer:** Identifies the relevant time expressions.
3. **Apply / demonstrate:** Write one new sentence for each time category using an accurate time expression. This task assesses: “ใช้คำระบุเวลาที่แสดงเหตุการณ์ในอดีต ปัจจุบัน และอนาคต”. **Answer:** Sentences use past, present and future references consistently.
### 54. SO4.1-P2-02

- **Standard:** SO4.1
- **2021 priority classification:** should_know
- **OBEC pathway classification:** between_learning
- **Official indicator:** ลำดับเหตุการณ์ที่เกิดขึ้นในครอบครัว หรือในชีวิตของตนเองโดยใช้หลักฐานที่เกี่ยวข้อง

**Objective:** `SO4.1-P2-02` — ลำดับเหตุการณ์ที่เกิดขึ้นในครอบครัว หรือในชีวิตของตนเองโดยใช้หลักฐานที่เกี่ยวข้อง
- **Learning intention:** Learners will demonstrate: ลำดับเหตุการณ์ที่เกิดขึ้นในครอบครัว หรือในชีวิตของตนเองโดยใช้หลักฐานที่เกี่ยวข้อง
**Suggested teaching sequence**

1. Display an age-appropriate historical image, object or event prompt. Ask learners what they notice and what they still need evidence to establish.
2. Model how to identify source type, author/context, date and relevant detail. Separate what the source directly shows from an inference or opinion.
3. Learners sequence events or compare two sources, recording details that support a cause, consequence, change, continuity or historical interpretation relevant to the indicator.
4. Each learner writes, explains or presents a short conclusion supported by source evidence. Check chronology and whether the evidence actually supports the claim.

**Resources:** a dated photograph or artefact image, a short primary/secondary source extract and a timeline or source-analysis frame.

**Differentiation:** For early primary learners: Use large pictures, concrete objects, short oral instructions and teacher modelling; allow learners to point, sort, draw or answer orally.

**Learning intention:** Learners will be able to demonstrate the specific knowledge or performance in this official indicator: ลำดับเหตุการณ์ที่เกิดขึ้นในครอบครัว หรือในชีวิตของตนเองโดยใช้หลักฐานที่เกี่ยวข้อง

**Practical activity:** Use an age-appropriate historical source, image or timeline; identify evidence, sequence events and distinguish evidence from opinion. Adjust the complexity to early primary learners and use a source or response format appropriate to their age.

**Expected evidence:** Three completed responses demonstrate SO4.1-P2-02: accurate identification, a clear explanation with a relevant example, and a grade-appropriate application task. Accept oral, written, visual, map-based, timeline-based or practical evidence where it measures the same target.

**Teacher check:** Check separately (1) accuracy against the Thai indicator, (2) explanation supported by an example or source, and (3) application to the scenario. Give feedback on the missing criterion.

**Assessment stimulus — local-history evidence set:** Use a permission-cleared or fictional family sequence with dated photographs, records or interview notes; each item must have an explicit source label.

**Independent practice — indicator-specific assessment tasks**
1. **Recall / evidence:** Identify two events and the evidence that records each one. This assesses: “ลำดับเหตุการณ์ที่เกิดขึ้นในครอบครัว หรือในชีวิตของตนเองโดยใช้หลักฐานที่เกี่ยวข้อง”. **Answer:** Events and source references match the records.
2. **Explain / compare:** Arrange the events in chronological order and explain which date or source clue supports the order. This task assesses: “ลำดับเหตุการณ์ที่เกิดขึ้นในครอบครัว หรือในชีวิตของตนเองโดยใช้หลักฐานที่เกี่ยวข้อง”. **Answer:** Correct order with an explicit evidence clue.
3. **Apply / demonstrate:** Create a three-event timeline and label the evidence source beside every event. This task assesses: “ลำดับเหตุการณ์ที่เกิดขึ้นในครอบครัว หรือในชีวิตของตนเองโดยใช้หลักฐานที่เกี่ยวข้อง”. **Answer:** Timeline is chronological and every entry has a source label; fictional cases are acceptable.
### 55. SO4.2-P2-01

- **Standard:** SO4.2
- **2021 priority classification:** should_know
- **OBEC pathway classification:** between_learning
- **Official indicator:** สืบค้นถึงการเปลี่ยนแปลงในวิถีชีวิตประจำวันของคนในชุมชนของตนจากอดีตถึงปัจจุบัน

**Objective:** `SO4.2-P2-01` — สืบค้นถึงการเปลี่ยนแปลงในวิถีชีวิตประจำวันของคนในชุมชนของตนจากอดีตถึงปัจจุบัน
- **Learning intention:** Learners will demonstrate: สืบค้นถึงการเปลี่ยนแปลงในวิถีชีวิตประจำวันของคนในชุมชนของตนจากอดีตถึงปัจจุบัน
**Suggested teaching sequence**

1. Display an age-appropriate historical image, object or event prompt. Ask learners what they notice and what they still need evidence to establish.
2. Model how to identify source type, author/context, date and relevant detail. Separate what the source directly shows from an inference or opinion.
3. Learners sequence events or compare two sources, recording details that support a cause, consequence, change, continuity or historical interpretation relevant to the indicator.
4. Each learner writes, explains or presents a short conclusion supported by source evidence. Check chronology and whether the evidence actually supports the claim.

**Resources:** a dated photograph or artefact image, a short primary/secondary source extract and a timeline or source-analysis frame.

**Differentiation:** For early primary learners: Use large pictures, concrete objects, short oral instructions and teacher modelling; allow learners to point, sort, draw or answer orally.

**Learning intention:** Learners will be able to demonstrate the specific knowledge or performance in this official indicator: สืบค้นถึงการเปลี่ยนแปลงในวิถีชีวิตประจำวันของคนในชุมชนของตนจากอดีตถึงปัจจุบัน

**Practical activity:** Use an age-appropriate historical source, image or timeline; identify evidence, sequence events and distinguish evidence from opinion. Adjust the complexity to early primary learners and use a source or response format appropriate to their age.

**Expected evidence:** Three completed responses demonstrate SO4.2-P2-01: accurate identification, a clear explanation with a relevant example, and a grade-appropriate application task. Accept oral, written, visual, map-based, timeline-based or practical evidence where it measures the same target.

**Teacher check:** Check separately (1) accuracy against the Thai indicator, (2) explanation supported by an example or source, and (3) application to the scenario. Give feedback on the missing criterion.

**Assessment stimulus — local-history evidence set:** Use dated photographs, interview extracts or local records about one community practice or daily-life feature in the past and present. Identify source and date.

**Independent practice — indicator-specific assessment tasks**
1. **Recall / evidence:** Identify one feature of community life in the older source and one in the current source. This assesses: “สืบค้นถึงการเปลี่ยนแปลงในวิถีชีวิตประจำวันของคนในชุมชนของตนจากอดีตถึงปัจจุบัน”. **Answer:** Correctly identifies both time periods and features.
2. **Explain / compare:** Explain one change and one continuity using evidence from both sources. This task assesses: “สืบค้นถึงการเปลี่ยนแปลงในวิถีชีวิตประจำวันของคนในชุมชนของตนจากอดีตถึงปัจจุบัน”. **Answer:** One accurate change and one continuity supported by the sources.
3. **Apply / demonstrate:** Describe how one change has affected daily life in the community, citing the sources. This task assesses: “สืบค้นถึงการเปลี่ยนแปลงในวิถีชีวิตประจำวันของคนในชุมชนของตนจากอดีตถึงปัจจุบัน”. **Answer:** Effect is plausible and evidence-based; avoid unsupported generalisations.
### 56. SO4.2-P2-02

- **Standard:** SO4.2
- **2021 priority classification:** should_know
- **OBEC pathway classification:** terminal
- **Official indicator:** อธิบายผลกระทบของการเปลี่ยนแปลงที่มีต่อวิถีชีวิตของคนในชุมชน

**Objective:** `SO4.2-P2-02` — อธิบายผลกระทบของการเปลี่ยนแปลงที่มีต่อวิถีชีวิตของคนในชุมชน
- **Learning intention:** Learners will demonstrate: อธิบายผลกระทบของการเปลี่ยนแปลงที่มีต่อวิถีชีวิตของคนในชุมชน
**Suggested teaching sequence**

1. Display an age-appropriate historical image, object or event prompt. Ask learners what they notice and what they still need evidence to establish.
2. Model how to identify source type, author/context, date and relevant detail. Separate what the source directly shows from an inference or opinion.
3. Learners sequence events or compare two sources, recording details that support a cause, consequence, change, continuity or historical interpretation relevant to the indicator.
4. Each learner writes, explains or presents a short conclusion supported by source evidence. Check chronology and whether the evidence actually supports the claim.

**Resources:** a dated photograph or artefact image, a short primary/secondary source extract and a timeline or source-analysis frame.

**Differentiation:** For early primary learners: Use large pictures, concrete objects, short oral instructions and teacher modelling; allow learners to point, sort, draw or answer orally.

**Learning intention:** Learners will be able to demonstrate the specific knowledge or performance in this official indicator: อธิบายผลกระทบของการเปลี่ยนแปลงที่มีต่อวิถีชีวิตของคนในชุมชน

**Practical activity:** Use an age-appropriate historical source, image or timeline; identify evidence, sequence events and distinguish evidence from opinion. Adjust the complexity to early primary learners and use a source or response format appropriate to their age.

**Expected evidence:** Three completed responses demonstrate SO4.2-P2-02: accurate identification, a clear explanation with a relevant example, and a grade-appropriate application task. Accept oral, written, visual, map-based, timeline-based or practical evidence where it measures the same target.

**Teacher check:** Check separately (1) accuracy against the Thai indicator, (2) explanation supported by an example or source, and (3) application to the scenario. Give feedback on the missing criterion.

**Assessment stimulus — local-history evidence set:** Use two dated, verified sources showing a change in a community practice or living condition, plus a short account from a person affected.

**Independent practice — indicator-specific assessment tasks**
1. **Recall / evidence:** Identify the change shown in the two sources. This assesses: “อธิบายผลกระทบของการเปลี่ยนแปลงที่มีต่อวิถีชีวิตของคนในชุมชน”. **Answer:** Identifies the change accurately.
2. **Explain / compare:** Explain one positive or negative effect on a named group/person in the account. This task assesses: “อธิบายผลกระทบของการเปลี่ยนแปลงที่มีต่อวิถีชีวิตของคนในชุมชน”. **Answer:** Effect matches the testimony or record and names who was affected.
3. **Apply / demonstrate:** Compare the effect on two groups or activities, using evidence and noting any limitation in the sources. This task assesses: “อธิบายผลกระทบของการเปลี่ยนแปลงที่มีต่อวิถีชีวิตของคนในชุมชน”. **Answer:** Comparison is supported and does not claim more than the sources show.
### 57. SO4.3-P2-01

- **Standard:** SO4.3
- **2021 priority classification:** should_know
- **OBEC pathway classification:** between_learning
- **Official indicator:** ระบุบุคคลที่ทำประโยชน์ต่อท้องถิ่นหรือประเทศชาติ

**Objective:** `SO4.3-P2-01` — ระบุบุคคลที่ทำประโยชน์ต่อท้องถิ่นหรือประเทศชาติ
- **Learning intention:** Learners will demonstrate: ระบุบุคคลที่ทำประโยชน์ต่อท้องถิ่นหรือประเทศชาติ
**Suggested teaching sequence**

1. Display an age-appropriate historical image, object or event prompt. Ask learners what they notice and what they still need evidence to establish.
2. Model how to identify source type, author/context, date and relevant detail. Separate what the source directly shows from an inference or opinion.
3. Learners sequence events or compare two sources, recording details that support a cause, consequence, change, continuity or historical interpretation relevant to the indicator.
4. Each learner writes, explains or presents a short conclusion supported by source evidence. Check chronology and whether the evidence actually supports the claim.

**Resources:** a dated photograph or artefact image, a short primary/secondary source extract and a timeline or source-analysis frame.

**Differentiation:** For early primary learners: Use large pictures, concrete objects, short oral instructions and teacher modelling; allow learners to point, sort, draw or answer orally.

**Learning intention:** Learners will be able to demonstrate the specific knowledge or performance in this official indicator: ระบุบุคคลที่ทำประโยชน์ต่อท้องถิ่นหรือประเทศชาติ

**Practical activity:** Use an age-appropriate historical source, image or timeline; identify evidence, sequence events and distinguish evidence from opinion. Adjust the complexity to early primary learners and use a source or response format appropriate to their age.

**Expected evidence:** Three completed responses demonstrate SO4.3-P2-01: accurate identification, a clear explanation with a relevant example, and a grade-appropriate application task. Accept oral, written, visual, map-based, timeline-based or practical evidence where it measures the same target.

**Teacher check:** Check separately (1) accuracy against the Thai indicator, (2) explanation supported by an example or source, and (3) application to the scenario. Give feedback on the missing criterion.

**Assessment stimulus — local-history evidence set:** Use a verified profile of a person who contributed to the local area or country, with a reliable record of the contribution and source attribution.

**Independent practice — indicator-specific assessment tasks**
1. **Recall / evidence:** Name the person and identify the contribution recorded in the profile. This assesses: “ระบุบุคคลที่ทำประโยชน์ต่อท้องถิ่นหรือประเทศชาติ”. **Answer:** Correct person and documented contribution.
2. **Explain / compare:** Explain who benefited and how, using evidence from the source. This task assesses: “ระบุบุคคลที่ทำประโยชน์ต่อท้องถิ่นหรือประเทศชาติ”. **Answer:** Beneficiary and effect are supported by the profile.
3. **Apply / demonstrate:** Write a short profile caption that distinguishes the documented contribution from personal opinion. This task assesses: “ระบุบุคคลที่ทำประโยชน์ต่อท้องถิ่นหรือประเทศชาติ”. **Answer:** Caption is accurate and separates fact from opinion.
### 58. SO4.3-P2-02

- **Standard:** SO4.3
- **2021 priority classification:** must_know
- **OBEC pathway classification:** terminal
- **Official indicator:** ยกตัวอย่างวัฒนธรรม ประเพณีและภูมิปัญญาไทยที่ภาคภูมิใจและควรอนุรักษ์ไว้

**Objective:** `SO4.3-P2-02` — ยกตัวอย่างวัฒนธรรม ประเพณีและภูมิปัญญาไทยที่ภาคภูมิใจและควรอนุรักษ์ไว้
- **Learning intention:** Learners will demonstrate: ยกตัวอย่างวัฒนธรรม ประเพณีและภูมิปัญญาไทยที่ภาคภูมิใจและควรอนุรักษ์ไว้
**Suggested teaching sequence**

1. Display an age-appropriate historical image, object or event prompt. Ask learners what they notice and what they still need evidence to establish.
2. Model how to identify source type, author/context, date and relevant detail. Separate what the source directly shows from an inference or opinion.
3. Learners sequence events or compare two sources, recording details that support a cause, consequence, change, continuity or historical interpretation relevant to the indicator.
4. Each learner writes, explains or presents a short conclusion supported by source evidence. Check chronology and whether the evidence actually supports the claim.

**Resources:** a dated photograph or artefact image, a short primary/secondary source extract and a timeline or source-analysis frame.

**Differentiation:** For early primary learners: Use large pictures, concrete objects, short oral instructions and teacher modelling; allow learners to point, sort, draw or answer orally.

**Learning intention:** Learners will be able to demonstrate the specific knowledge or performance in this official indicator: ยกตัวอย่างวัฒนธรรม ประเพณีและภูมิปัญญาไทยที่ภาคภูมิใจและควรอนุรักษ์ไว้

**Practical activity:** Use an age-appropriate historical source, image or timeline; identify evidence, sequence events and distinguish evidence from opinion. Adjust the complexity to early primary learners and use a source or response format appropriate to their age.

**Expected evidence:** Three completed responses demonstrate SO4.3-P2-02: accurate identification, a clear explanation with a relevant example, and a grade-appropriate application task. Accept oral, written, visual, map-based, timeline-based or practical evidence where it measures the same target.

**Teacher check:** Check separately (1) accuracy against the Thai indicator, (2) explanation supported by an example or source, and (3) application to the scenario. Give feedback on the missing criterion.

**Assessment stimulus — local-history evidence set:** Use verified examples of a Thai cultural practice, tradition or local wisdom, including who practises it, where it occurs and why community members value it.

**Independent practice — indicator-specific assessment tasks**
1. **Recall / evidence:** Name one cultural practice or local-wisdom example in the source and identify where it is practised. This assesses: “ยกตัวอย่างวัฒนธรรม ประเพณีและภูมิปัญญาไทยที่ภาคภูมิใจและควรอนุรักษ์ไว้”. **Answer:** Correct example and context.
2. **Explain / compare:** Explain why the source says it is valued or worth preserving. This task assesses: “ยกตัวอย่างวัฒนธรรม ประเพณีและภูมิปัญญาไทยที่ภาคภูมิใจและควรอนุรักษ์ไว้”. **Answer:** Reason is supported by the source or attributed community voice.
3. **Apply / demonstrate:** Suggest one respectful way young people could help sustain or document it, with a reason. This task assesses: “ยกตัวอย่างวัฒนธรรม ประเพณีและภูมิปัญญาไทยที่ภาคภูมิใจและควรอนุรักษ์ไว้”. **Answer:** Practical, respectful proposal that does not treat culture as unchanging or uniform.
### 59. SO5.1-P2-01

- **Standard:** SO5.1
- **2021 priority classification:** should_know
- **OBEC pathway classification:** terminal
- **Official indicator:** ระบุสิ่งแวดล้อมทางธรรมชาติและที่มนุษย์สร้างขึ้น ซึ่งปรากฏระหว่างบ้านกับโรงเรียน

**Objective:** `SO5.1-P2-01` — ระบุสิ่งแวดล้อมทางธรรมชาติและที่มนุษย์สร้างขึ้น ซึ่งปรากฏระหว่างบ้านกับโรงเรียน
- **Learning intention:** Learners will demonstrate: ระบุสิ่งแวดล้อมทางธรรมชาติและที่มนุษย์สร้างขึ้น ซึ่งปรากฏระหว่างบ้านกับโรงเรียน
**Suggested teaching sequence**

1. Begin with a place, landscape, route or environmental issue familiar to learners. Locate it and ask what spatial information would help explain it.
2. Model how to read the map, key, scale, direction, photograph or data display needed for the indicator. Demonstrate the geographic terms accurately.
3. Pairs identify a location, pattern or relationship and use at least two visible details from the map, image or data to explain it.
4. Learners produce an annotated map, short interpretation or evidence-based response. Assess correct use of geographic evidence and the link between people and place.

**Resources:** a labelled map or atlas extract, a local photograph, a simple map/data set and a geographic vocabulary frame.

**Differentiation:** For early primary learners: Use large pictures, concrete objects, short oral instructions and teacher modelling; allow learners to point, sort, draw or answer orally.

**Learning intention:** Learners will be able to demonstrate the specific knowledge or performance in this official indicator: ระบุสิ่งแวดล้อมทางธรรมชาติและที่มนุษย์สร้างขึ้น ซึ่งปรากฏระหว่างบ้านกับโรงเรียน

**Practical activity:** Use a local map, photograph, spatial data or environmental case; identify location/pattern, explain the relationship and propose an evidence-based action. Adjust the complexity to early primary learners and use a source or response format appropriate to their age.

**Expected evidence:** Three completed responses demonstrate SO5.1-P2-01: accurate identification, a clear explanation with a relevant example, and a grade-appropriate application task. Accept oral, written, visual, map-based, timeline-based or practical evidence where it measures the same target.

**Teacher check:** Check separately (1) accuracy against the Thai indicator, (2) explanation supported by an example or source, and (3) application to the scenario. Give feedback on the missing criterion.

**Assessment stimulus — school/community geography field card:** Use a route map or photo sequence between home and school, distinguishing natural and human-made features along the route.

**Independent practice — indicator-specific assessment tasks**
1. **Recall / evidence:** Identify one natural and one human-made feature between home and school in the source. This assesses: “ระบุสิ่งแวดล้อมทางธรรมชาติและที่มนุษย์สร้างขึ้น ซึ่งปรากฏระหว่างบ้านกับโรงเรียน”. **Answer:** Correctly classifies both features.
2. **Explain / compare:** Explain how each feature affects movement or daily life along the route. This task assesses: “ระบุสิ่งแวดล้อมทางธรรมชาติและที่มนุษย์สร้างขึ้น ซึ่งปรากฏระหว่างบ้านกับโรงเรียน”. **Answer:** Explains a plausible effect on movement or daily life.
3. **Apply / demonstrate:** Describe one way to travel or behave safely in relation to a feature shown. This task assesses: “ระบุสิ่งแวดล้อมทางธรรมชาติและที่มนุษย์สร้างขึ้น ซึ่งปรากฏระหว่างบ้านกับโรงเรียน”. **Answer:** Safety advice matches the feature and context; a fictional route is acceptable.
